# ECCO-DarwinDiff — research map

**Read this before proposing, designing or launching anything.** It is the index of what the
project already knows and how strongly, organised by inference mode over evidence, claims and
open hypotheses.

**Why it exists.** On 2026-07-30 one session re-derived work the repo already contained **four
separate times** (the GHG Center assessment twice, the rain-ratio precedence, the 1-D column
study), one at a cost of 1.52M tokens. Nothing indexed *questions already answered*. §1 is the fix.

**How it was built.** 25 agents over all 169 documents in `docs/findings` and
`docs/research_notes`: 12 extracted structured rows, 12 adversarially re-read the cited documents
to refute bad ones, and the result was merged and deduplicated. **Chunk 5's verifier died on a
connection error**, so rows sourced only from
`2026-07-10_iron_partitioning_breaks_the_wall`, `2026-07-19_v05_chlorophyll_vs_modis`,
`2026-07-22_column_osse_result`, `2026-07-22_two_anchor_redteam`,
`2026-07-23_observable_frontier_config_analysis`, `2026-07-24_bgc_operator_lineage_synthesis`,
`2026-07-28_bling_comparability`, `2026-07-29_contract_prior_art`,
`2026-07-30_daily_emulator_artifact_is_contaminated` and
`2026-07-30_scavrat_is_locally_identifiable_in_the_southern_ocean` are **extract-only, not
independently verified**. Treat them with the same caution as an unverified claim.

**Query it, do not just read it:** `python scripts/research_map_db.py settled <term>`. See
CLAUDE.md for the full harness.

---

## 0. Schema

```
EVIDENCE   (ev_id, kind, locator, job, gate, n, date)
CLAIM      (cl_id, statement, mode, status, doc, parameter, track)
SUPPORTS   (cl_id, ev_id, relation)        relation ∈ {establishes, qualifies, refutes}
HYPOTHESIS (hy_id, statement, predicts, falsifier, prereg, status)
SUPERSEDES (old_cl, new_cl, reason)
```

| mode | answers | strong when | fails when |
|---|---|---|---|
| **deductive** | what *must* follow from structure | the derivation is checkable by inspection | a premise is wrong |
| **inductive** | what *does* happen, repeatedly | it has n **and** a matched untrained null | no null, small n, or selection over configs |
| **abductive** | what best *explains* a surprise | it predicts something it was not built to explain | it explains only what prompted it |

The working loop is **abduction → deduction → pre-registration → induction**. §8 is a worked
example that completed on 2026-07-31.

---

## 8. Worked example: the loop that closed on 2026-07-31

1. **Abduction** (2026-07-22). A 0-D box cannot identify `scav_rat`, CRLB(log scav) = 4.2e6.
   *Best explanation:* the vertical profile carries it, `alpfe` sets the profile **amplitude**
   and `scav_rat` sets its **shape**. Recorded on **synthetic** self-twin data.
2. **Deduction.** If that holds then surface-only iron must fail for `scav_rat` and succeed for
   `alpfe`, and subsurface-only must do the reverse. Forced, not guessed.
3. **Pre-registration** (2026-07-30). Decision rule and falsifier fixed with zero JSONs on disk.
4. **Induction** (2026-07-31). `so_surf` 14/50 against `so_sub` 33/50, Fisher exact
   P = 2.68e-4, `verify_run` exit 0, and the falsifier cleared (`alpfe` 49/50 in `so_surf`).

**Repeat this pattern.** The strength came from step 2 producing a prediction the mechanism was
*not* built to explain, and from step 3 happening before step 4.
